

tamiḻp pēravic cemmal
veḷḷivāraṇaṉār


nūl varici - 13



tolkāppiyam - poruḷatikāram
puṟattiṇi iyal



āciriyar
pērāciriyar ka.veḷḷivāraṇaṉār

nūṟkuṟippu

patippuri
19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutippakutiyum, 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkappakutiyum tamiḻppulamiyum tamiḻāyvum mēlōṅki vaḷarnta poṟkālamākum. ip poṟkālap pakutiyiltāṉ tamiḻppēravic cemmal peruntamiḻaṟiñar ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār tōṉṟi vaḷarntu vāḻntu tāymoḻit tamiḻukku vaḷamum valimiyum cērttār.
ipperum pēraṟiñar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum veḷiyiṭa muṭivuceytu ka.veḷḷivāraṇaṉār nūl varici eṉum talippil 21 tokutikaḷ mutal kaṭṭamāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. kikkuk kiṭikkap peṟāta nūlkaḷit tēṭiyeṭuttu iṉivarum kālaṅkaḷil veḷiyiṭa muyalvōm. 
 tamiḻ ici, tamiḻ ilakkiyam, tamiḻ ilakkaṇam, civa cittāntam ākiya nālvakit tuṟikaḷi miyamākak koṇṭu ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum perum payaṉtarak kūṭiya aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷākum. āḻnta camayappaṟṟāḷar, patavikkum pukaḻukkum kācukkum tammi āṭpaṭuttik koḷḷātu tamiḻppaṇi oṉṟiyē tam vāḻviṉ mūccākak koṇṭu vāḻntavar,  naṭuvaṇaracu tamiḻakattil kalavimoḻiyām intiyik (1938) kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākat tamiḻnāṭṭuk kalvi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil pukuttiyapōtu ataṉi etirttup pōrpparaṇi pāṭiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷil ivarum oruvar.  kākki viṭutūtu eṉum inti etirppu nūli eḻuti aṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭu āṭcik kaṭṭilil mutalvarāka amarntirunta irācācikku aṉuppit tam etirppip pativu ceytavar. 
tammuṭiya tamiḻāyvuppaṇi mūlam tamiḻ varalāṟṟil nilittu niṟpavar,  tamiḻiyum civattiyum iru kaṇkaḷeṉak koṇṭavar.   tamiḻilakkaṇat toṉṉūlām tolkāppiyatti  yum, piṉṉūlām naṉṉūliyum āḻntakaṉṟu kaṟṟu oppāyvu ceytavar, tam karuttukaḷum vāḻkki muṟiyum muraṇpaṭāmal eṇṇiyatic colli, colliyapaṭi naṭimuṟiyil vāḻntu kāṭṭiya peruntamiḻaṟiñar.  
tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟa peyar iyaṟpeyarē eṉṟu niṟuviyavar, tolkāppiyar kālattil vaṭakkē vēṅkaṭamalittoṭarum, teṟkē kumariyāṟum tamiḻaka ellikaḷāka amintiruntaṉaveṉṟum, kaṭalkōḷukkup piṟaku kumarikkaṭal teṉ elli āṉatu eṉpatiyum, tolkāppiyar iṭiccaṅkak kālattavar, tolkāppiyam iṭiccaṅkak kālattil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, muccaṅka varalāṟṟi mutaṉmutalil kūṟiyatu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uritāṉ eṉpatiyum, tolkāppiyam, caṅkac ceyyuḷukkum tirukkuṟaḷukkum neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉṉarē iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, tirumūlar tam tirumantiramē cittānta cāttiram patiṉāṉkiṟkum mutal nūlāka tikaḻvatu eṉpatiyum, tirumuṟi kaṇṭa cōḻaṉ mutalām irācarācaṉ alla mutalām ātittaṉē  tirumuṟi kaṇṭa cōḻaṉ eṉpatiyum,  vaḷḷalāriṉ tiruvaruṭpā tamiḻiṉ colvaḷamum, poruḷ nuṭpamum, oppaṟṟap pēraruḷiṉ iṉpamum niṟintatu  eṉpatiyum, civa camayam āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, paḻantamiḻ nākarikattiṉ ūṟṟukkaṇ tamiḻum civamum eṉpatiyum tam nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi uṟuti ceytavar. tam āyvup pulamiyāl pala putiya ceytikaḷiyum tamiḻ ulakukku aḷittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻulakiṟkup perumi cērppaṉa. tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟiṟku  kūṭutal varavāka amivaṉa. ivaruṭiya aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷa ṉittiyum āvaṇappaṭuttum   nōkkuṭaṉ tokuttu tamiḻ ulakiṟku vippākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. itaṉi veḷikkoṇara emakkut tuṇiyāyirunta em patippakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ, nūlkaḷ koṭuttu utaviyavarkaḷ,  kaṇiṉi, meyppu, accu, nūl kaṭṭamippu ceytu innūlvarici ceppamuṭaṉ  veḷivarat tuṇiniṉṟa aṉivarukkum naṉṟi.  em tamiḻk kāppup paṇikku tuṇi niṟka vēṇṭukiṟōm.
2010	 patippakattār












mutal patippuri
maturi kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakam tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattiṟku aminta ellāvurikaḷiyun tokuttu uriviḷakkakkuṟippukkaḷōṭum āyvuriyuṭaṉum veḷiyiṭum paṇiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭuḷatu. tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārat tiṉ mutaliyalākiya akattiṇiyiyal urivaḷam vittuvāṉ mu. aruṇācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ eḻutiya uriviḷakkattōṭum āyvuriyuṭaṉum 1975-il veḷiyiṭappeṟṟatu. ataṉit toṭarntu puṟattiṇiyiyal urivaḷam ippoḻutu veḷiyiṭappeṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉkaṇ tolkāppiyap puṟattiṇiyiyalukku iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum eḻutiyuḷḷa paḻiya vurikaḷuṭaṉ kaṇakkāyar nāvalar ṭākṭar ca. cōmacuntara pāratiyāravarkaḷ ārāynteḻutiya tolkāppiyap poruṭ paṭalap puṟattiṇiyiyaṟ putturiyum cūttirantōṟum kāla aṭivil tokuttamikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvurikaḷip payilvōr urikaḷilaminta aruñcoṟporuḷkaḷiyum uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ koḷkikaḷiyum teḷivākavuṇarntu koḷḷutaṟku vāyppāka avvavvurikaḷiyoṭṭiya uriviḷakkaṅkaḷ aṭikkuṟippākak koṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. cūttirantōṟum aminta im mūṉṟurikaḷiṉ piṉṉē āyvuri eṉṟa pakuti cērkkap peṟṟuḷatu. itu nūlāciriyarākiya tolkāppiyaṉār iyaṟṟiya mūlatti yaṭiyoṟṟic cūttirattiṉ karuttum poruḷum teḷivākap pulappaṭumpaṭi eḷiyanaṭiyil eḻutap peṟṟatākum.
tamiḻnūlkaḷip payilvōr tamiḻmuṉṉōr vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻeṇkaḷi maṟavātu payaṉpaṭuttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum nōkkattuṭaṉ ivvurivaḷappatippil cūttira eṇkaḷum pakka eṇkaḷum tamiḻeṇkaḷākavē amikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tolkāppiyappuṟattiṇiyiyaliluḷḷa tuṟikaḷākap puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉavum, puṟap poruḷ veṇpā māliyiṟ putiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭuvaṉavum ākiya puṟattiṇittuṟikaḷ innūliṉ piṟcērkkiyil aṭṭavaṇip paṭuttit tarappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
puṟattiṇit tuṟikaḷukkuriya ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka uri yāciriyarkaḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷa utāraṇappāṭalkaḷ aṭiyaḷavil mikkaṉa. avaṟṟi avvāṟē veḷiyiṭuvatāyiṉ, nūliṉ pakkaṅkaḷ mikum accuttāḷ viliyēṟiya ikkālattil puttakattiṉ viliyum paṉmaṭaṅkāka uyarum. eṉavē uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ urivēṟupāṭukaḷi oppunōkkiyārāyum nōkkuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭappeṟum ivvurivaḷappatippil utāraṇac ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ mutaṟkuṟippum nūṟpeyarum pāṭaleṇṇumaṭṭum tarappeṟṟaṉa. uriyāciriyarkaḷ utāraṇappāṭalkaḷiṉ toṭar kaḷiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭi viḷakkantarum iṭaṅkaḷil appāṭalkaḷ uriyiluḷḷavāṟu muḻuvatum veḷiyiṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
innūl varicikaḷ, tamiḻiyaṟpulattiṉar ceytumuṭitta āyvuppaṇikaḷiṉ payaṉāka veḷivaruvaṉa. ivi toṭarntu nikaḻtaṟkum veḷivarutaṟkum uṟutuṇiyāy viḷaṅkiya muṉṉāḷ tuṇivēntar ṭākṭar va. cupa. māṇikkam, innāḷ tuṇivēntar pērāciriyar jē. irāmaccantiraṉ ākiyōrkkum palkalik kaḻaka āṭcikkuḻuviṉarkkum naṉṟi kūṟum kaṭap pāṭuṭiyēṉ.


innūlkaḷ tamiḻ mutukali māṇākkarkaṭkum āyvāḷar kaṭkum peritum payaṉpaṭuvaṉavātaliṉ kallūri nūlakaṅkaḷ tōṟum ip palkalik kaḻaka veḷiyīṭukaḷ vāṅki vikkap peṟutal ciṟappuṭiyatākum. tamiḻ ārvalar aṉivarukkum ivvuri vaḷam ciṟanta maṉavaḷattit tamiḻ āyvuvaḷattip perukkumeṉpatu uṟuti.
ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉ








tolkāppiyam - poruḷatikāram
urivaḷam
mukavuri
ulakiyal vaḻakkum ilakkiyat toṉmiyum orucēra vāykkappeṟṟa uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyākat tikaḻvatu tamiḻ eṉpataṉi aṟiñar palarum naṉkuṇarvar. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka makkaḷatu pēccu moḻiyākavum ceyyuḷ moḻiyākavum vaḻaṅkappeṟṟuvarum tamiḻmoḻi, kālantōṟum ulakiyal vāḻvil ēṟpaṭum palvēṟu iṭiyūṟukaṭkuṭ paṭṭut taṉatu uruttiriyātu iṟappu etirvu nikaḻvu eṉṉum mukkālattiṟkum ottut toṭarum nilipēṟuṭiyatākavum uyartiṇi makkaḷukkuk kaṇṇeṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum kalvi vaḷarcciyiṟ pala vēṟu kalittuṟikaṭkum nilikkaḷamāy virintu iṭaṅkoṭukkum coṟparappuṭiyatākavum nam muṉṉōrkaḷāl ilakkaṇavarampuṭaṉ pōṟṟi vaḷarkkap peṟṟuḷatu.
ivvāṟu ekkālattum nilipēṟuṭiya iṉiya eḷiya ilakkaṇavarampukōlit tamiḻmoḻiyip pōṟṟi vaḷartta pulamic cāṉṟōrkaḷil mutaṟkaṇvittu eṇṇattakka talimic ciṟappum toṉmiyum vāyntavar āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār eṉpataṉit tamiḻulakam naṉkuṇarum. tolkāppiyaṉār iyaṟṟiya iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlākiya tol kāppiyam tamiḻmoḻikku ekkālattum amiyavēṇṭiya iyalpākiya vaḷarcci neṟikaḷi maṉattuṭkoṇṭu iyaṟṟap peṟṟatākum. eḻuttiṉ tiṟaṉālum colvaḷattālum vaḻakkuñceyyuḷumākiya iruvaki vaḻakku neṟikaḷālum tamiḻmoḻi paṇṭik kālattil evvāṟu ilakkaṇa varampukōli vaḷarkkappeṟṟatu eṉpataṉi ikkālattārum teḷivākavuṇarntu piṉpaṟṟutaṟ kuriya iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇa nūlākat tikaḻvatu ittolkāppiyam oṉṟēyākum. piṟkālattil tōṉṟiya iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli, yāpparuṅkalam, vīracōḻiyam, naṉṉūl, nēminātam, tamiḻneṟiviḷakkam, akapporuḷ viḷakkam mutaliya tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ yāvum ittolkāppiyap poruḷi yuḷattuṭkoṇṭu iyaṟṟappeṟṟaṉavēyeṉpatu annūlkaḷit tolkāppiyattuṭaṉ oppunōkki ārāy vārkku iṉitu pulaṉām.
tolkāppiyattil eḻuttuñ collum poruḷumeṉa vakippaṭutti viḷakkappeṟum ulakavaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkumākiya moḻinaṭi paṟṟiya tamiḻilakkaṇa vitikaḷik kūrntu nōkkuṅkāl, avaṟṟukkellām nilikkaḷamākat tolkāppiyaṉār kālattiṟku muṉ ettuṇiyō ciṟanta pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ amippiṉi viḷakkum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum tamiḻmoḻiyil nilipeṟṟu vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉām. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār tam kālattilum tamakku muṉṉum iyaṟṟappeṟṟu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻilakkaṇa ilakkiya nūlkaḷiyellām naṉku ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ vitikaḷiyellāntokuttut tolkāppiyamākiya innūliyiyaṟṟi yutaviṉār eṉpatu,
		vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
āyiṭit
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉ
eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭic
centami ḻiyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu
muntu nūlkaṇṭu muṟippaṭa eṇṇip
pulantokut tōṉē
eṉap paṉampāraṉār pāṭiya ciṟappup pāyirap pakutiyāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ eḻuttuc coṟporuḷ eṉṉum pākupāṭṭiṉ iyaṟkiyamippiṉic ciṟitum citiyātu pātukākkum uyarnta kuṟikkōḷuṭaṉ iyaṟṟappeṟṟa tol kāppiyamākiya innūl, paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ amippiṉiyum piṟkālattil tōṉṟi vaḻaṅkum palvēṟu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyāta kūṟupāṭukaḷiyum taṉpāṟ koṇṭu tikaḻum taṉic ciṟappuṭiya muḻumi vāynta iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇa nūlākum. innūl iṭic caṅkattārkkum kaṭic caṅkattārkkum ilakkaṇa nūlāy viḷaṅkiya teṉpataṉi muccaṅkaṅkaḷiṉ varalāṟu kūṟumiṭattu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluri yāciriyar teḷivāka viḷakkiyuḷḷami kāṇalām.
kaḷappirar, pallavar mutaliya ayal maṉṉarkaḷiṉ paṭi yeṭuppiṉ kāraṇamākat tamiḻakattil ēṟpaṭṭa araciyal māṟutalkaḷālum nāṭṭil maḻiyiṉmiyāṟ pallāṇṭukaḷ toṭarntu varuttiya pañcattiṉālum tamiḻmakkaḷ tamiḻ vaḷarcciyiṟ cōrvuṟṟamiyāl tamiḻiyal nūlākiya tolkāppiyattiṉ payiṟci pāṇṭināṭṭil iṭikkālattiṟ kuṉṟiyateṉpatu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal muṉṉuriyālum
		vaṭuvilkāppiya maturivāypporuḷ
	marapu vīṭṭiyatāl
vaḻutiyāṭciyi vaḷavaṉ māṟṟiṭa
	maturi kūppiṭunāḷ
aṭivukōttaṉa amutacūttiram
	aṟupatāyc caminūl
amarar kīḻppaṭa aṟiñarmēṟpaṭa
	aruḷu mūrttikaḷē

eṉavarum oṭṭakkūttar vāymoḻiyālum oruvāṟu uyttuṇarappaṭum. tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpillāta ayalavar camutāya vāḻvil mēlōṅkiya piṟṟināḷil tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ toṉmi nākarikattōṭu poruntāta koḷkikaḷ cila toṉmi yuṭiyaṉa pōlat  tamiḻ makkaḷatu vāḻvil pukuttap peṟṟamiyāl akkaruttukkaḷiṟ cila tolkāppiya marapiyalil nāḷaṭivil iṭampeṟuvaṉavāyiṉa.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻ makkaḷatu vāḻkki muṟi ayalavar kūṭṭuṟavāṟ citintu māṟiya piṟkālattil tolkāppiyattiṉ poruḷ marapiṉi uḷḷavāṟaṟiyum vāyppum nāḷaṭiviṟ kuṟintu pōyiṟṟu. ittakiya iṭarniliyiṉum tamiḻartam vāḻviyal nūlākiya tolkāppiyattiṟkup poruḷkaṇṭu teḷintu urivarinta perumi uriyāciriyar eṉap pōṟṟap peṟum iḷampūraṇavaṭikaḷukkēyuriyatākum. iḷampūraṇa aṭikaḷip pōlavē pērāciriyar, cēṉāvariyar, kallāṭar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, teyvacciliyār mutaliya perumakkaḷ cilar tolkāppiyatti varaṉmuṟiyākap payiṉṟu nuṇporuḷ kaṇṭu urivarintuḷḷaṉar. eṉiṉum ivarkaḷ eḻutiyavuri tolkāppiyam muḻuvataṟkum kāṇap paṭavilli. iḷampūraṇarip pōṉṟu pērāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum tolkāppiyam muḻuvataṟkum uri varintiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu avviruvaruṭiya uri kaḷiyum kūrntu nōkkuṅkāṟ pulaṉākum, iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum piṟavuriyāciriyar urikaḷit tammurikaḷiṟ kuṟippiṭutalāl avviruvar kālattiṟku muṉṉum tol kāppiyattukkup palvēṟurikaḷ vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉak karuta vēṇṭiyuḷatu.
tolkāppiya vuriyāciriyarkaḷākiya ipperiyōrkaḷ tamakkup paṉṉūṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟiya tol kāppiyaṉārkālat tamiḻar nākarikattiṉiyum tamiḻakattil iṭik kālattil vantu pukunta ayalavar kalappiṉāluḷatākiya piṟkālac cātivēṟṟumi paṟṟiya camutāya māṟṟattiṉiyum vēṟu pirittuṇarum vāyppiṉip peṟṟārallar. eṉiṉum tamakku iyalpāka aminta kūrttamatiyiṉālum tolkāppiya ilakkaṇavarampiṉi naṉkuṇarntu pāṭappeṟṟa pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭuttoki tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliya paṇṭittamiḻ nūlkaḷit tuṟipōkap payiṉṟu uṇarnta teḷiviṉālum tol kāppiyattiṟku nūlāciriyar karuttuṇarntu meypporuḷ kāṇuntiṟattiṟ peritum veṟṟi peṟṟārkaḷ eṉpatiṟ ciṟitum aiyamilli.
tamiḻakam tamiḻmūvēntar āṭciyiyiḻantu ayalavarāṭcik kuṭpaṭṭa iṭikkālattil, makkaḷatu vāḻviyal kalvi nākarikam kaṭavuḷvaḻipāṭu mutaliya ellāt tuṟikaḷilum ayalavaratu moḻiyum camutāya amippumē mītūrntu niṉṟamiyāl innāṭṭiṟ paraviya ayalavar nākarikam tamiḻ makkaḷatu ulakap potumiyuṇarviṉiyum avartam toṉmi nākarikattiṉiyum ettuṇik kūrtta matiyāḷarum vēṟu pirittuṇarntu koḷḷa iyalātavāṟu maṟittuviṭṭa teṉṟē collalām. iṅṅaṉam tamiḻar nākarikam ayalavar kūṭṭuṟavāṟ pirittuṇara viyalāta niliyeytiya piṟkālattilē vāḻnta uriyāciriyarkaḷ tam kālac cūḻniliyi yoṭṭit tolkāppiyattukku urikaṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷum cilavuḷa. tolkāppiyap poruḷati kārattiṟku iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum eḻutiya urippakutikaḷi oppiṭṭu ārāyvārkku ivvuṇmi pulaṉām.
tolkāppiyavuriyāciriyarkaḷ kālac cūḻniliyi yoṭṭit tolkāppiya urikaḷil iṭampeṟṟa ayalavar koḷkikaḷi iviyeṉa ārāyntu vilakki, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār kālattoṉmiyiṉiyuḷaṅkoṇṭu tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārap pakutikkup putturi kāṇa vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṭki aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻt talimiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭa pērāciriyar nāvalar 
ca. cōmacuntarapāratiyāravarkaḷ uḷḷattē mukiḻtteḻuntatu. ataṉ payaṉākat tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārap pakutikkup putturi kāṇum paṇi avarkaḷāl toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. tolkāppiyattil akattiṇiyiyal, puṟattiṇiyiyal, meyppāṭṭiyal ākiya mūṉṟiyalkaḷukku nāvalar pāratiyār avarkaḷ putturi varintuḷḷārkaḷ.
tamiḻiṉ eḻuttu, col āciyavaṟṟiṉ amippiṉiyum poruḷpulappāṭṭu neṟiyiṉiyum oppa viḷakkum iyaṟṟamiḻt toṉṉūṟporuḷi uḷḷavāṟuṇarntu tamiḻ moḻiyiṉip pēṇivaḷarttaṟkum paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷatu akam puṟam eṉṉum vāḻviyalamippiṉit tolkāppiyamākiya ilakkaṇattōṭum caṅka ilakkiyam tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliya toṉmiyilakkiyaṅkaḷōṭum oppunōkkiyuṇartaṟkum peruntuṇipurintuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyattiṟku iḷam pūraṇar, pērāciriyar mutaliya perumakkaḷ ārāynteḻutiya paḻiyavurikaḷākum. tolkāppiyam payilvōr annūluk kaminta ellāvurikaḷiyum varalāṟṟu muṟiyil orucērat tokuttu uriyāciriyarkaḷ kūṟum urivēṟupāṭukaḷip pakuttu ārāyntu nūlāciriyarākiya tolkāppiyaṉār karutiya poruḷ ituvāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉat teḷintuṇarum muṟiyil tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattukku ōr urivaḷappatippu veḷiyiṭutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉpataṉi aṟiñar palarum naṉkuṇarvar. tamiḻ nilipeṟṟa maturimānakaril tōṉṟiya maturi kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakam ittamiḻp paṇiyiṉi 1966il toṭaṅkiyatu.
aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattuṟiyil muppatāṇṭu kaḷukku mēṟ paṇiyāṟṟi ōyvupeṟṟu maturi cōmacuntarapuram kuṭiyiruppil vāḻntavarum eṉṉuṭiya āciriyap peruntakiyum ākiya vittuvāṉ mu. aruṇācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ, intiyap palkalikkaḻakap poruḷutavikkuḻu palkalikkaḻakattiliruntu ōyvupeṟṟa āciriyarkaḷukkeṉa amitta āyvuttiṭṭattiṉ aṭippaṭiyil maturippalkalikkaḻakattilamarntu tol kāppiyap poruḷatikāra urivaḷamākiya ippulamip paṇiyiṉit toṭaṅkic ciṟanta muṟiyil nikaḻtti vantārkaḷ. tolkāppiyapporuḷatikārattiṉ mutaliyal ākiya akattiṇiyiyalukku iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum eḻutiyavurikaḷ iraṇṭum eṉatu vaṇakkattukkum pōṟṟu talukkum uriya pērāciriyar kaṇakkāyar nāvalar ṭākṭar ca. cōmacuntarapāratiyār avarkaḷ eḻutiya putturiyum eṉa mūṉṟu urikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. vittuvāṉ mu. aruṇācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ akattiṇiyiyalukkuk kiṭittuḷḷa im mūṉṟurikaḷiyum cūttirantōṟum ārāyntu, kālamuṟip paṭiamittu iṉṟiyamiyāta uri viḷakkaṅkaḷi avvavvurikaḷ tōṟum aṭikkuṟippākat tantu avaṟṟiṉ muṭivil accūttirap poruḷi virittu viḷakkum muṟiyil tamatu āyvuriyiṉi yamittu akattiṇiyiyal uri vaḷattiṉi niṟivu ceytuḷḷārkaḷ; tiru. piḷḷiyavarkaḷatu ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāka uruvākiya akattiṇiyiyal urivaḷam 1972-il maturippalkalikkaḻakat tamiḻttuṟiyiṉ cārpil veḷiyiṭappeṟṟatu.
taṇṇār tamiḻaḷikkun teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ tali nakarākiya maturimānakariṉ pāṅkart tōṉṟip palvēṟu kalit tuṟikaḷiyum tamiḻmoḻi vāyilākap parappum paṇiyiṉit taṉatu kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭa maturi kāmarācar palkalik kaḻakam tamiḻt toṉṉūlkaḷi muṟippaṭa ārāyntu āyvu nūlkaḷ pala veḷiyiṭum nōkkuṭaṉ tamiḻttuṟiyiṉ kiḷaviyākat tamiḻiyaṟpuṟam (TAMILOLOGY) eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ tamiḻārāyccit tuṟiyiṉi iraṇṭāṇṭukaṭkumuṉ niṟuviyatu. palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷilum kallūrikaḷiluḷ pallāṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟi ōyvupeṟṟa aṟiñar cilari oppanta muṟiyil āyvāḷarāka niyamittut tamiḻārāycci toṭarntu nikaḻa vaḻiyamittuḷḷatu. tamiḻiyaṟ pulattu āyvāḷarum oruvarāka niyamikkappeṟṟa yāṉ eṉṉuṭiya āciriyap peruntaki vittuvāṉ mu. aruṇācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷāl toṭaṅkappeṟṟa tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāra urivaḷappaṇiyiṉit toṭarntu puṟat tiṇiyiyal mutal uvamaviyal īṟākavuḷḷa āṟiyalkaḷukkuriya urikaḷit tokukkum paṇiyiṉi niṟivuceytuḷḷēṉ. tolkāppiyattiṟkuk kiṭikkum paḻiyavurikaḷuḷ toṉmivāynta  iḷampūraṇaruri mutaṟkaṇṇum ataṉi yaṭuttuk kiṭikkum ēṉiyavurikaḷ ataṉpiṉṉum ākak kālamuṟippaṭi cūttirantōṟum pakuttu amikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ovvōruriyiṉkīḻum urippakutikkuriya iṉṟiyamiyāta viḷakkaṅkaḷ aṭikkuṟippākat tarappeṟṟaṉa. uriyāciriyarkaḷ eḻutiya urippakutikaḷuḷ nūlāciriyarkaruttuṭaṉ ottu ēṟṟukkoḷḷaṟpālaṉavum muraṇpaṭuvaṉavum iviyeṉa aṭikkuṟippil uriyaviṭaṅkaḷil viḷakkappeṟṟaṉa. cūttiran tōṟum paḻiyavurikaḷil muṭivil tolkāppiya mūlattiyaṭi yoṟṟi variyappeṟṟa āyvuri cāyveḻuttil amikkap peṟṟuḷatu.
accuttāḷvili mikuntuḷḷa innāḷil uri yāciriyarkaḷ uriyiṟ kāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷa utāraṇap pāṭalkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum muḻuvaṭiyil uḷḷapaṭiyē veḷiyiṭu vatāṉāl puttakattiṉ vilimikutiyākum eṉpataṉālum tolkāppiyattiṟ kaminta urivēṟupāṭukaḷi oppunōkki āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār cūttirattiṟ collakkarutiya poruḷ ituveṉa ārāyntu teḷintuṇarum muṟiyil āyvāḷarkkup payaṉpaṭu muṟiyil veḷiyiṭappeṟuvatu ivvārāyccip patippātalāṉum urikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum utāraṇap pāṭalkaḷiṉ mutaṟkuṟippum nūṟpeyarum pāṭal eṇṇum curukkit tarappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. uriyāciriyarkaḷ tām eṭuttukkoṇṭa ilakkaṇattip pulappaṭuttaṟkuriya ilakkiyap poruḷamitiyiṉiyō piṟa kuṟippukkaḷiyō utāraṇap pāṭalkaḷiliruntu eṭuttuk kāṭṭi viḷakkantarumiṭaṅkaḷil avvap pāṭaṟpakutikaḷ muḻuvatum tarappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
ovvoru cūttiravurikaṭkum muṭivil aminta āyvurip pakuti muṉṉuḷḷa paḻiyavurikaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ aṭikkuṟippukkaḷākat tarappeṟṟuḷḷa uri viḷakkaṅkaḷiyum taḻuviya niliyil mēlum taṭiviṭikaḷukkiṭaṉiṉṟit tolkāppiya mūlatti aṭiyoṟṟi eḷiyanaṭiyil teḷivuriyāka variyappeṟṟatākum.
ivvāṟu orunūlukku aminta ellāvurikaḷiyum tokuttu veḷiyiṭap peṟuvatākiya urivaḷappatippu civa cittānta cāttiram patiṉāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟākiya civañāṉa cittiyār cupakkattiṟkuk koṉṟamānakaram caṇmukacuntara mutaliyār avarkaḷāl kali 4991-il (ki.pi. 1890) veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu. ivvurivaḷappatippil civañāṉacittiyār cupakkattukku maṟi ñāṉatēcikar, civākkirayōkikaḷ, nirampa vaḻakiya tēcikar, ñāṉappirakācar, civañāṉamuṉivar, cuppiramaṇiya tēcikar ākiya aṟuvarum eḻutiya urikaḷ pāṭalkaḷ tōṟum pakuttu amikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. immuṟiyiṉip piṉpaṟṟi makāvittuvāṉ tiru. ca. taṇṭapāṇitēcikar avarkaḷ tirukkuṟaḷukkuk kiṭikkum ellāvurikaḷiyun tokuttut tamatu āyvuk kuṟippukkaḷuṭaṉ tirukkuṟaḷ urivaḷattiṉit tarumiyātīṉattiṉ veḷiyīṭāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ immuṟiyiṉi yaṭiyoṟṟiyē nālaṭiyārkkup patumaṉār mutaliyōr eḻutiya urikaḷit tokuttu nālaṭiyār urivaḷam eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ tañcic caracuvatimakāl nūl niliyattār iraṇṭu tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. ippoḻutu tamiḻppalkalik kaḻakattiṉ tuṇivēntarāka amarntu kalvippaṇipuriyum ṭākṭar va.ay. ivvāṟu orunūlukkaminta urikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum kāla aṭivil varicippaṭutti iṉṟiyamiyāta uri viḷakkaṅkaḷuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭappeṟum urivaḷappatippu mūlanūliṉ āciriyar karutiya poruḷiyum ataṟkaminta urikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum viḷakkaṅkaḷiyum uḷḷavāṟu kaṟṟuṇara virumpuvārkkum apporuḷ kuṟittu mēlum ārāya virumpum ārāycciyāḷarkkum peritum tuṇipurivatākum.
iṭiccaṅkattārkkum kaṭiccaṅkattārkkum ilakkaṇa nūlāy viḷaṅkiya toṉmi vāyntatu iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇa nūlākiya tolkāppiyam eṉpataṉi aṟiñar palarum naṉkuṇarvar. innūl, tāṉ tōṉṟiya nāḷmutal tamiḻ moḻiyiṉiyum tamiḻar nākarikattiṉiyum pēṇi vaḷarkkum putumippolivuṭaṉ tikaḻntuvarum perumiyuṭiyatākum. innūlik kālantōṟum āḻntu payiṉṟa pulamic celvarkaḷ innūlukku varintuḷḷa urikaḷ yāvum kaṭiccaṅka kāla mutal iṉṟaḷavum palvēṟu parappuṭiyaṉavākak kiḷittu vaḷarnta tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟukkum varampākat tikaḻum muḻumutal ilakkaṇam ittolkāppiyamēyeṉṉum uṇmiyiṉi vaṟpuṟuttum niliyil amintuḷḷaṉa. ittakiya urivaḷaṅkaḷāl tamiḻ mutaṉūlākiya tol kāppiyattiṟkup palvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil vāḻnta pulavar perumakkaḷ kaṇṭuṇarttiya poruḷ nuṭpaṅkaḷit tokuttuk kāṇum vāyppu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. paṇṭit tamiḻt toṉṉūlukku meyyuri kāṇun tiṟattil tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ kālantōṟum peṟṟuḷḷa karuttu vaḷarcciyiṉiyum avvavvuriyāciriyarkaḷ vāḻnta kālac cūḻnilikaḷiyum uḷḷavāṟu uṇaramuṭikiṉṟatu. palvēṟu urikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum maṟuppum taṭiviṭikaḷuṅ koṇṭu mūla nūlāciriyar collakkarutiya poruḷ ituvākat tāṉ iruttal vēṇṭum eṉat tuṇitaṟkuriya nūṟcāṉṟukaḷum kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāram kūṟum akattiṇi puṟattiṇi paṟṟiya tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ vāḻviyal oḻukalāṟukaḷum avaṟṟip pāṭutaṟkuriya pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākiya ceyyuḷamippum avi kuṟitta coṟporuḷ marapukaḷum naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa.
maturi kāmarācar palkalik kaḻakattiṉ tuṇivēntarāka amarntu, palkalikkaḻakam aṟiviyal, poṟiyiyal, toḻiliyal mutaliya palatuṟikaḷilum intiyap palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷuḷ mutaṉmipeṟṟuviḷaṅkutal vēṇṭum eṉṉum peruviruppuṭaṉ ayarātu uḻittum tamiḻāyvukkeṉat tamiḻiyaṟpulam eṉṉum taṉittuṟiyiṉi amittum tamiḻ nalam pēṇiya centamiḻc cemmal ṭākṭar va. cupa. māṇikkam em.ē.piec. o.,o.È£, avarkaḷāvar. tamiḻiyaṟpulattiṉ āyvāḷaruḷ oruvarāka eṉṉi aḻittu iṇippāḷarāka niyamittup paṇikoṇṭa palkalikkaḻakat tuṇivēntarākiya avarkaṭkum palkalikkaḻaka āṭcikkuḻuviṉarkkum eṉatu uḷamārnta naṉṟiyiṉi aṉpuṭaṉ terivittukkoḷkiṉṟēṉ. em iraṇṭāṇṭup paṇiyiṉ payaṉāṉa nūlkaḷ veḷivarum iccamayattil, avi ciṟappuṭaṉ veḷivara uṟutuṇi nalki varum tuṇivēntar pērāciriyar jē. irāmaccantiraṉ avarkaṭkum eṉ uḷamārnta naṉṟi uriyatākum.
tamiḻiyaṟpulattiṉ paṇikaḷ iṉitu nikaḻa avvappoḻutu āvaṉapurintutaviya tamiḻttuṟit talivarum tamiḻiyal tuṟi maṟṟum intiya moḻittuṟikaḷiṉ iṇippāḷarum ākiya ṭākṭar. irāma. bgÇafU¥g‹ (jÄH©zš) v«.V.,ãv¢.o. avarkaṭku eṉatu naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatākum. tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāra uri vaḷattiṉi veḷiyiṭuvatil ūkkamum utaviyum nalkivarum palkalikkaḻakanūṟ patippāḷarum iṇippērāciriyarum ākiya ṭākṭar katir. kfhnjt‹ v«.v.,v«.È£.,ãv¢.o avarkaṭkum ivvuri vaḷappatippukku uṟutuṇipurinta tamiḻiyaṟpulattuk kalveṭṭuttuṟi āyvāḷar pērāciriyar tiru. ci. kōvintarācaṉār avarkaṭkum eṉatu naṉṟiyuriyatākum. ï›îiu ts¥gâ¥ã‹ ifbaG¤J¥ gofis muhJ M®tKl‹ j£l¢R¢ brŒJjÉa âU brštfnzr‹, ã.rÆ., nūliṉ accuppaṭiyiṉit tiruttiyutaviya tiru. ta. nrhkRªju« v«.V., ākiya aṉparkaṭku eṉṉuṭiya aṉpārnta pārāṭṭum vāḻttum eṉṟum uriyavākum. innūl ceṉṉiyil accēṟiyapōtu accuppaṭikaḷic cīrceyta ṭākṭar. ci. pālacuppiramaṇiyaṉ avarkaṭku eṉṉuṭiya naṉṟiyiyum pārāṭṭiyum terivippatil makiḻcci aṭikiṟēṉ.
ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉ


mutal patippuri
maturi kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakam tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattiṟku aminta ellāvurikaḷiyun tokuttu uriviḷakkakkuṟippukkaḷōṭum āyvuriyuṭaṉum veḷiyiṭum paṇiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭuḷatu. tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārat tiṉ mutaliyalākiya akattiṇiyiyal urivaḷam vittuvāṉ mu. aruṇācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷ eḻutiya uriviḷakkattōṭum āyvuriyuṭaṉum 1975-il veḷiyiṭappeṟṟatu. ataṉit toṭarntu puṟattiṇiyiyal urivaḷam ippoḻutu veḷiyiṭappeṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉkaṇ tolkāppiyap puṟattiṇiyiyalukku iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum eḻutiyuḷḷa paḻiya vurikaḷuṭaṉ kaṇakkāyar nāvalar ṭākṭar ca. cōmacuntara pāratiyāravarkaḷ ārāynteḻutiya tolkāppiyap poruṭ paṭalap puṟattiṇiyiyaṟ putturiyum cūttirantōṟum kāla aṭivil tokuttamikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvurikaḷip payilvōr urikaḷilaminta aruñcoṟporuḷkaḷiyum uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ koḷkikaḷiyum teḷivākavuṇarntu koḷḷutaṟku vāyppāka avvavvurikaḷiyoṭṭiya uriviḷakkaṅkaḷ aṭikkuṟippākak koṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. cūttirantōṟum aminta im mūṉṟurikaḷiṉ piṉṉē āyvuri eṉṟa pakuti cērkkap peṟṟuḷatu. itu nūlāciriyarākiya tolkāppiyaṉār iyaṟṟiya mūlatti yaṭiyoṟṟic cūttirattiṉ karuttum poruḷum teḷivākap pulappaṭumpaṭi eḷiyanaṭiyil eḻutap peṟṟatākum.
tamiḻnūlkaḷip payilvōr tamiḻmuṉṉōr vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻeṇkaḷi maṟavātu payaṉpaṭuttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum nōkkattuṭaṉ ivvurivaḷappatippil cūttira eṇkaḷum pakka eṇkaḷum tamiḻeṇkaḷākavē amikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tolkāppiyappuṟattiṇiyiyaliluḷḷa tuṟikaḷākap puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉavum, puṟap poruḷ veṇpā māliyiṟ putiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭuvaṉavum ākiya puṟattiṇittuṟikaḷ innūliṉ piṟcērkkiyil aṭṭavaṇip paṭuttit tarappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
puṟattiṇit tuṟikaḷukkuriya ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka uri yāciriyarkaḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷa utāraṇappāṭalkaḷ aṭiyaḷavil mikkaṉa. avaṟṟi avvāṟē veḷiyiṭuvatāyiṉ, nūliṉ pakkaṅkaḷ mikum accuttāḷ viliyēṟiya ikkālattil puttakattiṉ viliyum paṉmaṭaṅkāka uyarum. eṉavē uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ urivēṟupāṭukaḷi oppunōkkiyārāyum nōkkuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭappeṟum ivvurivaḷappatippil utāraṇac ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ mutaṟkuṟippum nūṟpeyarum pāṭaleṇṇumaṭṭum tarappeṟṟaṉa. uriyāciriyarkaḷ utāraṇappāṭalkaḷiṉ toṭar kaḷiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭi viḷakkantarum iṭaṅkaḷil appāṭalkaḷ uriyiluḷḷavāṟu muḻuvatum veḷiyiṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
innūl varicikaḷ, tamiḻiyaṟpulattiṉar ceytumuṭitta āyvuppaṇikaḷiṉ payaṉāka veḷivaruvaṉa. ivi toṭarntu nikaḻtaṟkum veḷivarutaṟkum uṟutuṇiyāy viḷaṅkiya muṉṉāḷ tuṇivēntar ṭākṭar va. cupa. māṇikkam, innāḷ tuṇivēntar pērāciriyar jē. irāmaccantiraṉ ākiyōrkkum palkalik kaḻaka āṭcikkuḻuviṉarkkum naṉṟi kūṟum kaṭap pāṭuṭiyēṉ.
innūlkaḷ tamiḻ mutukali māṇākkarkaṭkum āyvāḷar kaṭkum peritum payaṉpaṭuvaṉavātaliṉ kallūri nūlakaṅkaḷ tōṟum ip palkalik kaḻaka veḷiyīṭukaḷ vāṅki vikkap peṟutal ciṟappuṭiyatākum. tamiḻ ārvalar aṉivarukkum ivvuri vaḷam ciṟanta maṉavaḷattit tamiḻ āyvuvaḷattip perukkumeṉpatu uṟuti.
ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉ


tamiḻppatippu muṉṉōṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkuvōm!
tamiḻārāycciyiṉ vaḷarcciyil putiya pōkkukaḷi uṇṭākkiya perumikkuriyavarkaḷ paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷip patippitta aṟiñar kaḷēyāvar.  ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷil irunta ilakkiya, ilakkaṇapperuñ celvaṅkaḷi aṉivarum aṟiyumāṟu ceytu putiya āyviṟkut taṭam patittavarkaḷ ivarkaḷē āvar.
mēli nāṭṭār varukiyiṉāl tōṉṟiya acciyantira vacatikaḷum, kalvi maṟumalarcciyum putiya nūlākkaṅkaḷukku vaḻi vakuttaṉa. āṟumuka nāvalar (1822-1879) ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi (1832-1901), u.vē. cāminātiyar (1855-1942) ākiyōr tamiḻppatippu muṉṉōṭikaḷāy viḷaṅkit tamiḻukku vaḷam cērttaṉar eṉṟu tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakattiṉ mēṉāḷ tuṇivēntar, i. cuntaramūrtti taṉatu patippiyal cintaṉikaḷ eṉum nūlil pativu ceytuḷḷār. innūl tokutikaḷi veḷiyiṭuvataṉ mūlam tamiḻppatippu muṉṉōṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkuvōm.
